(p_variant.get_type() == Variant::NIL) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn true;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif (p_variant.get_type() != Variant::OBJECT) {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn false;\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\tbool was_freed = {\n\t\tERR_FAIL_COND(p_index < 0);\n\t\twhile (p_index >= container_element_types.size()) [Type System Interaction](#type-system-interaction)\n\n---\n\n## VM Architecture Overview\n\nThe GDScript VM is a **stack-based bytecode interpreter** with the following characteristics:\n\n### Execution Model\n\n- **Dispatch Method**: Uses computed goto (on GCC/Clang) for opcode dispatch, falling back to switch statements on other compilers\n- **Instruction Pointer (IP)**: Tracks the current position in the bytecode stream (in integers, not bytes)\n- **Stack Frame**: Each function call allocates a stack frame containing:\n  - Local variables (Variant types)\n  - Temporary values\n  - Instruction arguments\n  - Fixed addresses (self, class, nil)\n\n### Stack Layout\n\n```\n[Stack Frame]\n├── Fixed Addresses (3 slots)\n│   ├── ADDR_STACK_SELF (0): Object instance (self)\n│   ├── ADDR_STACK_CLASS (1): GDScript class reference\n│   └── ADDR_STACK_NIL (2): Nil value\n├── Arguments (parameter count)\n├── Local Variables\n└── Temporary Values\n\n[Instruction Arguments Area]\n├── Array of Variant* pointers for current instruction\n└── Used for multi-operand operations\n```\n\n### Key VM Properties\n\n- **Max Call Depth**: 2,048 (prevents stack overflow from infinite recursion)\n- **Address Bits**: 24 bits for address value + 2 bits for address type = 26 bits per address\n- **Variant Storage**: Variants are 24 bytes each on most platforms (12 bytes value + 12 bytes type info)\n\n---\n\n## Address Types and Operand Format\n\n### Address Type Classification\n\nEach operand in a bytecode instruction contains an address that specifies where data is located:\n\n```c\nenum Address {\n    ADDR_BITS = 24,                        // 24 bits for the address value\n    ADDR_MASK = ((1 << ADDR_BITS) - 1),   // Lower 24 bits = address index\n    ADDR_TYPE_MASK = ~ADDR_MASK,           // Upper bits = type\n\n    ADDR_TYPE_STACK = 0,      // Local variable, parameter, or temporary\n    ADDR_TYPE_CONSTANT = 1,   // Literal constant value\n    ADDR_TYPE_MEMBER = 2,     // Instance member variable\n    ADDR_TYPE_MAX = 3,        // Maximum number of address types\n};\n```\n\n### Address Encoding\n\nEach address operand is a 32-bit integer:\n\n```\n[Type (2 bits)] [Index (24 bits)]\n ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^  ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^\n Upper bits      Lower 24 bits\n\nType Field:\n  0b00 = ADDR_TYPE_STACK     (stack slot, constant, or temporary)\n  0b01 = ADDR_TYPE_CONSTANT  (constant pool reference)\n  0b10 = ADDR_TYPE_MEMBER    (instance member field)\n  0b11 = Reserved\n```\n\n### Extraction in VM\n\n```c\nint address = code_ptr[ip + offset];\nint address_type = (address & ADDR_TYPE_MASK) >> ADDR_BITS;\nint address_index = address & ADDR_MASK;\nVariant *target = &variant_addresses[address_type][address_index];\n```\n\n### Fixed Stack Addresses\n\nThese addresses are reserved and always present:\n\n```c\nenum FixedAddresses {\n    ADDR_STACK_SELF = 0,    // Slot 0: \"self\" (the instance)\n    ADDR_STACK_CLASS = 1,   // Slot 1: Class/Script object\n    ADDR_STACK_NIL = 2,     // Slot 2: nil literal\n    FIXED_ADDRESSES_MAX = 3,\n};\n\n// Convenience encodings\nADDR_SELF = ADDR_STACK_SELF | (ADDR_TYPE_STACK << ADDR_BITS)\nADDR_CLASS = ADDR_STACK_CLASS | (ADDR_TYPE_STACK << ADDR_BITS)\nADDR_NIL = ADDR_STACK_NIL | (ADDR_TYPE_STACK << ADDR_BITS)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Complete Opcode Reference\n\nThe GDScript bytecode instruction set contains **152 opcodes** (0-151).\n\n### Opcode Layout in Memory\n\nMost instructions follow this pattern:\n\n```\ncode[ip + 0]: Opcode (enum value)\ncode[ip + 1]: First operand address\ncode[ip + 2]: Second operand address (if needed)\ncode[ip + 3]: Third operand address (if needed)\ncode[ip + 4+]: Additional fields (operator type, method index, etc.)\n\nInstruction pointer advances by: 2 + number_of_operands + extra_data_size\n```\n\n### 1. Arithmetic & Type Operators\n\n#### `OPCODE_OPERATOR` (0)\n**Unvalidated binary operator (with type specialization)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_OPERATOR\ncode[ip+1]: operand_a (address)\ncode[ip+2]: operand_b (address)\ncode[ip+3]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+4]: operator_type (Variant::Operator enum)\ncode[ip+5]: cached_signature (operand types from last call)\ncode[ip+6]: cached_return_type\ncode[ip+7..7+PTR_SIZE]: cached_operator_function pointer\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Performs operations like +, -, *, /, %, ==, !=, <, >, <=, >=, etc.\n\n**Optimization**: On first execution, caches the operand type signature and operator function. On subsequent calls, if operand types match the cached signature, uses the fast path.\n\n**Sources**:\n- `modules/gdscript/gdscript_vm.cpp:691-776`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_OPERATOR_VALIDATED` (1)\n**Pre-validated binary operator (faster path)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_OPERATOR_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: operand_a (address)\ncode[ip+2]: operand_b (address)\ncode[ip+3]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+4]: operator_func_index (index into _operator_funcs_ptr)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Performs the same operations as OPCODE_OPERATOR but without runtime type checking.\n\n**When Used**: The compiler has verified operand types at compile time.\n\n**Sources**:\n- `modules/gdscript/gdscript_vm.cpp:778-793`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CAST_TO_BUILTIN` (28)\n**Cast to builtin type**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CAST_TO_BUILTIN\ncode[ip+1]: source_value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+3]: target_type (Variant::Type enum)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Converts a Variant to a specific builtin type (int, float, Vector3, etc.)\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CAST_TO_NATIVE` (29)\n**Cast to native Godot class**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CAST_TO_NATIVE\ncode[ip+1]: source_value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+3]: native_type_name_index (in _global_names_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CAST_TO_SCRIPT` (30)\n**Cast to GDScript or Script class**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CAST_TO_SCRIPT\ncode[ip+1]: source_value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address - should be a Script object)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. Type Testing (isinstance checks)\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_BUILTIN` (2)\n**Test if value is a specific builtin type**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_BUILTIN\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, stores bool)\ncode[ip+2]: value_to_test (address)\ncode[ip+3]: builtin_type (Variant::Type enum)\n```\n\n**Example**: `if value is int:`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_ARRAY` (3)\n**Test if value is a typed array**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, stores bool)\ncode[ip+2]: value_to_test (address)\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address)\ncode[ip+4]: element_builtin_type (Variant::Type)\ncode[ip+5]: element_native_type_index (_global_names_ptr index)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Tests both array type and element type: `if value is Array[int]:`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_NATIVE` (4)\n**Test inheritance from native class**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_NATIVE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, stores bool)\ncode[ip+2]: value_to_test (address)\ncode[ip+3]: native_type_index (_global_names_ptr index)\n```\n\n**Example**: `if node is Node2D:`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_SCRIPT` (5)\n**Test inheritance from GDScript class**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_SCRIPT\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, stores bool)\ncode[ip+2]: value_to_test (address)\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address - should be Script)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 3. Variable Assignment\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN` (20)\n**Copy assignment**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `dst = src`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_NULL` (21)\n**Assign nil**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_NULL\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `dst = null` (optimized for common case)\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TRUE` (22)\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_FALSE` (23)\n**Assign boolean literals**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_TRUE or OPCODE_ASSIGN_FALSE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_BUILTIN` (24)\n**Assign with type coercion to builtin**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_BUILTIN\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\ncode[ip+3]: target_type (Variant::Type)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Assignment with implicit conversion: `var i: int = \"123\"`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_ARRAY` (25)\n**Assign typed array with element type checking**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address)\ncode[ip+4]: element_builtin_type (Variant::Type)\ncode[ip+5]: element_native_type_index\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_NATIVE` (26)\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_SCRIPT` (27)\n**Assign with native/script type checking**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_NATIVE or OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_SCRIPT\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\ncode[ip+3]: type_info (address or index)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 4. Member Variable Access\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_MEMBER` (17)\n**Read instance member variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_MEMBER\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: member_index (integer, not address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `tmp = self.member_name`\n\n**Details**: Member index is used directly to access the `GDScriptInstance::members` array.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_MEMBER` (16)\n**Write instance member variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_MEMBER\ncode[ip+1]: member_index (integer)\ncode[ip+2]: value (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `self.member_name = value`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_STATIC_VARIABLE` (19)\n**Read static class variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_STATIC_VARIABLE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: static_var_index (in GDScript::static_variables)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_STATIC_VARIABLE` (18)\n**Write static class variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_STATIC_VARIABLE\ncode[ip+1]: static_var_index\ncode[ip+2]: value (address)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. Named Property Access (dictionaries, objects)\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_NAMED` (14)\n**Unvalidated property read**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_NAMED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: property_name_index (_global_names_ptr index)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `tmp = obj.property_name`\n\n**Validation**: Type checked at runtime for each access.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_NAMED_VALIDATED` (15)\n**Pre-validated property read (faster)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_NAMED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: property_name_index\ncode[ip+4]: getter_func_index (in _getters_ptr)\n```\n\n**When Used**: Compiler verified property exists on type.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_NAMED` (12)\n**Unvalidated property write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_NAMED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: property_name_index\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_NAMED_VALIDATED` (13)\n**Pre-validated property write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_NAMED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: property_name_index\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\ncode[ip+4]: setter_func_index (in _setters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6. Indexed Access (arrays, dictionaries)\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_KEYED` (9)\n**Unvalidated dictionary/array read with dynamic key**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_KEYED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: key (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `tmp = obj[key]` where key is any Variant\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_KEYED_VALIDATED` (10)\n**Pre-validated keyed read**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_KEYED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: key (address)\ncode[ip+4]: keyed_getter_index (in _keyed_getters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_INDEXED_VALIDATED` (11)\n**Pre-validated integer-indexed read (arrays)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_INDEXED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: index (address - should be int)\ncode[ip+4]: indexed_getter_index (in _indexed_getters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_KEYED` (6)\n**Unvalidated dictionary/array write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_KEYED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: key (address)\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_KEYED_VALIDATED` (7)\n**Pre-validated keyed write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_KEYED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: key (address)\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\ncode[ip+4]: keyed_setter_index (in _keyed_setters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_INDEXED_VALIDATED` (8)\n**Pre-validated integer-indexed write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_INDEXED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: index (address)\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\ncode[ip+4]: indexed_setter_index (in _indexed_setters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 7. Container Construction\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT` (31)\n**Construct builtin type instance**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: constructor_args via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\ncode[ip+3]: target_type (Variant::Type)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `vec3 = Vector3(1.0, 2.0, 3.0)`\n\n**Instruction Args Format**:\n```\ncode[ip+1]: arg_count (integer)\ncode[ip+2]: arg[0] (address)\ncode[ip+3]: arg[1] (address)\n...\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_VALIDATED` (32)\n**Pre-validated constructor with optimizations**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: args via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\ncode[ip+3]: constructor_func_index (in _constructors_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_ARRAY` (33)\n**Construct untyped Array**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: elements via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `arr = [1, 2, 3]`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_TYPED_ARRAY` (34)\n**Construct typed Array with element type**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_TYPED_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: elements via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address)\ncode[ip+4]: element_builtin_type (Variant::Type)\ncode[ip+5]: element_native_type_index\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_DICTIONARY` (35)\n**Construct Dictionary**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_DICTIONARY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: key_value_pairs via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `dict = {\"key\": value, ...}`\n\n**Instruction Args**: Alternating keys and values\n\n---\n\n### 8. Function Calls\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL` (36)\n**Unvalidated dynamic method call**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, for return value)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address, receiver)\ncode[ip+3]: method_name_index (_global_names_ptr index)\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `result = obj.method_name(arg1, arg2, ...)`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_RETURN` (37)\n**Return value from called method (awaitable)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_RETURN\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_name_index\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Like OPCODE_CALL but returns to caller immediately.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_ASYNC` (38)\n**Call method asynchronously (spawns a coroutine)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_ASYNC\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, for return value)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_name_index\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND` (43)\n**Call MethodBind-wrapped native method (slow path)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_bind_index (in _methods_ptr)\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**When Used**: Native class methods, slow path.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_RET` (44)\n**Call MethodBind with return handling**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_RET\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_bind_index\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_VALIDATED_RETURN` (46)\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_VALIDATED_NO_RETURN` (47)\n**Pre-validated MethodBind call (fast path)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_VALIDATED_*\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_bind_index\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**When Used**: Compiler verified the method exists and has correct signature.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_UTILITY` (40)\n**Call unvalidated utility (global) function**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_UTILITY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: utility_func_name_index (_global_names_ptr)\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `result = sin(x)`, `result = abs(value)`, etc.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_UTILITY_VALIDATED` (41)\n**Pre-validated utility function**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_UTILITY_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: utility_func_index (in _utilities_ptr)\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_GDSCRIPT_UTILITY` (42)\n**Call GDScript-specific utility function**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_GDSCRIPT_UTILITY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: gds_utility_func_index (in _gds_utilities_ptr)\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Example**: `print()`, `assert()`, etc.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_BUILTIN_TYPE_VALIDATED` (42)\n**Call builtin type method (pre-validated)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_BUILTIN_TYPE_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: builtin_method_index (in _builtin_methods_ptr)\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `string.to_upper()`, `array.push_back(x)`, etc.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_SELF_BASE` (45)\n**Call parent class method**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_SELF_BASE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: method_name_index\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `super.method()`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC` (49)\n**Call static native method**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: method_bind_index\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC_VALIDATED_RETURN` (50)\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC_VALIDATED_NO_RETURN` (51)\n**Pre-validated static native call**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC_VALIDATED_*\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: method_bind_index\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_BUILTIN_STATIC` (48)\n**Call static builtin type method**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_BUILTIN_STATIC\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: constructor_or_method_index\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n### 9. Lambda & Closure\n\n#### `OPCODE_CREATE_LAMBDA` (52)\n**Create anonymous function (closure)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CREATE_LAMBDA\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: lambda_func_index (in _lambdas_ptr)\ncode[ip+3]: capture_vars via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `lambda = func(x): return x + captured_var`\n\n**Captures**: Variables from enclosing scope are copied into lambda.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CREATE_SELF_LAMBDA` (53)\n**Create lambda capturing self**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CREATE_SELF_LAMBDA\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: lambda_func_index\ncode[ip+3]: capture_vars via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n### 10. Control Flow\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP` (54)\n**Unconditional jump**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP\ncode[ip+1]: target_ip (instruction pointer, absolute)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `goto label`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP_IF` (55)\n**Jump if condition is true**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP_IF\ncode[ip+1]: condition (address)\ncode[ip+2]: target_ip_true (absolute)\ncode[ip+3]: target_ip_false (absolute)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `if (condition) goto true_label; else goto false_label;`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP_IF_NOT` (56)\n**Jump if condition is false**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP_IF_NOT\ncode[ip+1]: condition (address)\ncode[ip+2]: target_ip_false (absolute)\ncode[ip+3]: target_ip_true (absolute)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP_TO_DEF_ARGUMENT` (57)\n**Jump to default argument initialization**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP_TO_DEF_ARGUMENT\ncode[ip+1]: target_ip\n```\n\n**Purpose**: When fewer arguments passed, jump to initialize remaining parameters.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP_IF_SHARED` (58)\n**Conditional jump for reference counting**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP_IF_SHARED\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: target_ip (if reference count > 1)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Copy-on-write optimization for containers.\n\n---\n\n### 11. Iteration\n\n#### `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_*` (64-82)\n**Initialize for-in loop iteration**\n\nType-specialized versions for each container type:\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN` (64) - Untyped/generic\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_INT` (65)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_FLOAT` (66)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_VECTOR2` (67)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_VECTOR2I` (68)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_VECTOR3` (69)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_VECTOR3I` (70)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_STRING` (71)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_DICTIONARY` (72)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_ARRAY` (73)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_PACKED_*_ARRAY` (74-82) - Various packed array types\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_OBJECT` (83) - Objects with _iterate()\n\n**Common Format**:\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_TYPE\ncode[ip+1]: iterator_var (address, stores iteration state)\ncode[ip+2]: container (address)\ncode[ip+3]: jump_target_if_done (absolute IP)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `for item in container:`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ITERATE_*` (84-102)\n**Continue for-in loop iteration**\n\nCorresponding OPCODE_ITERATE_* variants for each ITERATE_BEGIN_* type.\n\n**Format**:\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ITERATE_TYPE\ncode[ip+1]: iterator_var (address)\ncode[ip+2]: loop_variable (address, stores current item)\ncode[ip+3]: jump_target_if_done (absolute IP)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 12. Await (Coroutines)\n\n#### `OPCODE_AWAIT` (52)\n**Suspend execution waiting for signal/coroutine**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_AWAIT\ncode[ip+1]: signal_or_coroutine (address)\ncode[ip+2]: jump_target_on_resume (absolute IP)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `yield(object, \"signal\")` or `await signal.emitted`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_AWAIT_RESUME` (53)\n**Resume from await (called when signal is emitted)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_AWAIT_RESUME\ncode[ip+1]: result (address, stores resume value)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 13. Return Statements\n\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN` (59)\n**Return from function (no type checking)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_RETURN\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `return value`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_BUILTIN` (60)\n**Return with builtin type coercion**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_BUILTIN\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: return_type (Variant::Type)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_ARRAY` (61)\n**Return typed array**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: script_type (address)\ncode[ip+3]: element_builtin_type (Variant::Type)\ncode[ip+4]: element_native_type_index\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_NATIVE` (62)\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_SCRIPT` (63)\n**Return native/script typed value**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_*\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: type_info (address or index)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 14. Global Variables\n\n#### `OPCODE_STORE_GLOBAL` (103)\n**Store value to global variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_STORE_GLOBAL\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: global_var_index\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_STORE_NAMED_GLOBAL` (104)\n**Store to named global (by string name)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_STORE_NAMED_GLOBAL\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: global_name_index (_global_names_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 15. Type Adjustment (Implicit Conversions)\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_*` (105-149)\n**Implicit type conversion for all Godot types**\n\nType-specialized versions for each Variant type:\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_BOOL` (105)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_INT` (106)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_FLOAT` (107)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_STRING` (108)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_VECTOR2` (109)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_VECTOR2I` (110)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_RECT2` (111)\n- ... (up to OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_PACKED_VECTOR4_ARRAY)\n\n**Format**:\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_TYPE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Ensure a variable's type matches its declaration.\n\n---\n\n### 16. Debugging & Metadata\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSERT` (150)\n**Runtime assertion**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSERT\ncode[ip+1]: condition (address)\ncode[ip+2]: message (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `assert(condition, \"message\")`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_BREAKPOINT` (151)\n**Debugger breakpoint**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_BREAKPOINT\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Triggers debugger if attached.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_LINE` (152)\n**Line number metadata**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_LINE\ncode[ip+1]: line_number (integer)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Maps bytecode to source line for debugging.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_END` (153)\n**Marks end of bytecode**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_END\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Terminates function execution.\n\n---\n\n## Call Convention\n\n### Stack Frame Layout on Function Call\n\nWhen a GDScript function is called, the VM:\n\n1. **Allocates Stack Frame**:\n   ```\n   alloca_size = sizeof(Variant*) * 3              // Self, class, nil\n              + sizeof(Variant*) * _instruction_args_size  // Instr args\n              + sizeof(Variant) * _stack_size      // Local variables\n   ```\n\n2. **Initializes Fixed Addresses**:\n   ```c\n   stack[ADDR_STACK_SELF] = instance->owner    // \"self\"\n   stack[ADDR_STACK_CLASS] = script              // Class reference\n   stack[ADDR_STACK_NIL] = Variant()             // nil literal\n   ```\n\n3. **Places Arguments on Stack**:\n   ```c\n   for (int i = 0; i < p_argcount; i++) {\n       stack[i + 3] = arguments[i];  // Skip fixed addresses\n   }\n   ```\n\n4. **Initializes Local Variables**:\n   ```c\n   for (int i = p_argcount + 3; i < _stack_size; i++) {\n       new (&stack[i]) Variant();  // Call Variant constructor\n   }\n   ```\n\n5. **Initializes Temporaries** (if any):\n   ```c\n   for (const KeyValue<int, Variant::Type> &E : temporary_slots) {\n       type_init_function_table[E.value](&stack[E.key]);\n   }\n   ```\n\n### Parameter & Return Value Handling\n\n**Parameters**:\n- Stored at stack indices `[3, 3 + argument_count)`\n- Type-checked on entry if function declares types\n- Implicit conversions applied if needed\n\n**Default Arguments**:\n- If `argument_count > provided_argcount`, VM jumps to `OPCODE_JUMP_TO_DEF_ARGUMENT`\n- Code at that location initializes remaining parameters\n\n**Return Value**:\n- Stored in `Variant retvalue`\n- Returned by `OPCODE_RETURN*` instructions\n- Type-coerced if return type declared\n\n### Error Handling\n\n**Call Errors**:\n- `CALL_ERROR_TOO_FEW_ARGUMENTS` - Missing required parameters\n- `CALL_ERROR_TOO_MANY_ARGUMENTS` - Extra arguments passed\n- `CALL_ERROR_INVALID_ARGUMENT` - Type mismatch on parameter\n- `CALL_ERROR_INVALID_METHOD` - Method not found\n\n**Recursion Protection**:\n- `call_depth` counter (thread-local, max 2048)\n- Prevents stack overflow from infinite recursion\n\n### Instruction Arguments\n\nSome instructions (CALL, CONSTRUCT, etc.) use a special instruction arguments format:\n\n```\ncode[ip+1]: arg_count (number of arguments)\ncode[ip+2]: arg[0] address\ncode[ip+3]: arg[1] address\n...\n\nMacro: LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n  - Reads arg_count from code[ip+1]\n  - Populates instruction_args[] pointers\n  - Advances ip by 1 to skip arg_count field\n```\n\n**Usage in VM**:\n```c\n#define LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS           \\\n    int instr_arg_count = _code_ptr[ip + 1];    \\\n    for (int i = 0; i < instr_arg_count; i++) { \\\n        GET_VARIANT_PTR(v, i + 1);              \\\n        instruction_args[i] = v;                \\\n    }                                           \\\n    ip += 1;\n```\n\n---\n\n## Variable Access Patterns\n\n### Local Variables\n\n**Declaration**: Variables declared in function scope.\n\n**Bytecode Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nvar x: int = 10\n```\n\nCompiled to:\n```\nOPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_BUILTIN dst=[stack_index] src=[const_10] type=INT\n```\n\n**Memory**: Stored in stack frame at calculated offset.\n\n### Parameters\n\n**Declaration**: Function parameters.\n\n**Storage**: Stack slots `[3, 3 + param_count)`\n\n**Bytecode Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nfunc foo(a: int, b: Vector3):\n    pass\n```\n\nWhen called:\n- `stack[3]` = first argument (a)\n- `stack[4]` = second argument (b)\n- Arguments type-checked on entry\n\n### Member Variables\n\n**Declaration**: Instance member fields.\n\n**Access Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nself.member = value  # Write\nx = self.member       # Read\n```\n\n**Bytecode**:\n- Read: `OPCODE_GET_MEMBER dst=[stack] member_index=[field_index]`\n- Write: `OPCODE_SET_MEMBER member_index=[field_index] src=[stack]`\n\n**Memory**: Stored in `GDScriptInstance::members` array (Vector<Variant>)\n\n### Static Variables\n\n**Declaration**: Class-level static variables.\n\n**Access Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nstatic my_var = 10\nmy_var = 20\n```\n\n**Bytecode**:\n- Read: `OPCODE_GET_STATIC_VARIABLE dst=[stack] static_index=[var_index]`\n- Write: `OPCODE_SET_STATIC_VARIABLE static_index=[var_index] src=[stack]`\n\n**Memory**: Stored in `GDScript::static_variables` (Vector<Variant>)\n\n### Global Variables\n\n**Declaration**: Project globals (autoload singletons, constants).\n\n**Access Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nGLOBAL_VAR = value\nx = GLOBAL_VAR\n```\n\n**Bytecode**:\n- Write: `OPCODE_STORE_GLOBAL value=[stack] global_index=[index]`\n- Read: Compiled as constant folding or looked up at compile time\n\n### Temporary Variables\n\n**Purpose**: Hold intermediate values during expression evaluation.\n\n**Allocation**: `add_temporary()` in compiler\n\n**Lifetime**: From creation until next statement or cleanup point\n\n**Type Tracking**: Each temporary has a `Variant::Type` for optimization\n\n---\n\n## Type System Interaction\n\n### Variant Type Categories\n\nThe VM operates on Godot's `Variant` type system with these categories:\n\n#### Builtin Types\nPrimitive types with value semantics:\n- `BOOL`, `INT`, `FLOAT`, `STRING`\n- `VECTOR2`, `VECTOR3`, `VECTOR4`\n- `RECT2`, `AABB`, `PLANE`\n- `QUATERNION`, `TRANSFORM2D`, `TRANSFORM3D`, `PROJECTION`, `BASIS`\n- `COLOR`\n- `ARRAY`, `DICTIONARY` (container types)\n- `PACKED_BYTE_ARRAY`, `PACKED_INT32_ARRAY`, `PACKED_INT64_ARRAY`\n- `PACKED_FLOAT32_ARRAY`, `PACKED_FLOAT64_ARRAY`\n- `PACKED_STRING_ARRAY`, `PACKED_VECTOR2_ARRAY`\n- `PACKED_VECTOR3_ARRAY`, `PACKED_COLOR_ARRAY`, `PACKED_VECTOR4_ARRAY`\n\n#### Reference Types\nObject references with reference counting:\n- `OBJECT` - Generic Object pointer\n- `CALLABLE` - Function reference\n- `SIGNAL` - Signal reference\n- `STRING_NAME` - Interned string\n- `NODE_PATH` - Scene tree path\n- `RID` - Resource ID\n\n### Type Checking\n\n**Three-Level Validation**:\n\n1. **No Validation** (OPCODE_OPERATOR, OPCODE_CALL):\n   - Type checking done at runtime for each operation\n   - Slower but handles dynamic code\n\n2. **Compile-Time Validation** (OPCODE_OPERATOR_VALIDATED, OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_VALIDATED_*):\n   - Compiler verifies types match declarations\n   - Generates optimized bytecode with function pointers\n   - No runtime type checking needed\n\n3. **Implicit Conversion** (OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_*, OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_*):\n   - Automatic conversion between compatible types\n   - Example: `int x = 3.14` converts float to int\n\n### Type Annotation Storage\n\n**GDScriptDataType Structure**:\n```c\nstruct GDScriptDataType {\n    Kind kind;                    // BUILTIN, NATIVE, SCRIPT, GDSCRIPT\n    bool has_type;                // Was a type annotation present?\n    Variant::Type builtin_type;   // For BUILTIN kind\n    StringName native_type;       // For NATIVE kind\n    Script *script_type;          // For SCRIPT/GDSCRIPT kind\n    Vector<GDScriptDataType> container_element_types;  // For typed containers\n};\n```\n\n**Storage Locations**:\n- **Function parameters**: `GDScriptFunction::argument_types` (Vector<GDScriptDataType>)\n- **Return type**: `GDScriptFunction::return_type` (GDScriptDataType)\n- **Local variables**: Tracked during compilation in CodeGen\n\n### Typed Array Support\n\nArrays can have element types:\n\n**Declaration**:\n```gdscript\nvar arr: Array[Vector3]\nvar arr: Array[MyClass]\n```\n\n**Bytecode Operations**:\n- `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_TYPED_ARRAY` - Create with type\n- `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_ARRAY` - Assign with validation\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_ARRAY` - Test element type\n- `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_ARRAY` - Return with type coercion\n\n**Storage**:\n- Type info: `container_element_types[0]` in GDScriptDataType\n- Element builtin type, native type, or script type\n\n### Operator Validation\n\n**Dynamic Operator Resolution**:\n```c\n// OPCODE_OPERATOR flow:\n1. Check if signature cached (code[ip+5] != 0)\n2. If not cached:\n   - Get operand types: a->get_type(), b->get_type()\n   - Look up validated operator: Variant::get_validated_operator_evaluator(op, a_type, b_type)\n   - Initialize return value with correct type\n   - Cache signature and function pointer\n3. If cached and matches:\n   - Use fast path with cached function pointer\n4. If cached but doesn't match:\n   - Fall back to slower Variant::evaluate()\n```\n\n**Supported Operators**:\n- Arithmetic: `+`, `-`, `*`, `/`, `%`\n- Comparison: `==`, `!=`, `<`, `>`, `<=`, `>=`\n- Logical: `and`, `or`, `not`\n- Bitwise: `&`, `|`, `^`, `~`, `<<`, `>>`\n- Container: `in`, `[]` (indexing)\n- String: `+` (concatenation)\n- Type: `is` (type testing)\n\n---\n\n## Source Files Reference\n\nAll information in this document comes from analyzing the following Godot Engine source files:\n\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_function.h`** - Opcode enum, address types, GDScriptFunction class\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_vm.cpp`** - VM interpreter implementation, opcode dispatch, instruction execution\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_compiler.h`** - CodeGen structure for bytecode generation\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_byte_codegen.h`** - Bytecode generator implementation\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_compiler.cpp`** - Compiler logic\n\nSource: **Godot Engine 4.3-stable**\nhttps://godotengine.org\n\n---\n\n## Terminology\n\n- **IP (Instruction Pointer)**: Current position in bytecode stream (in integers)\n- **Stack Frame**: Memory region allocated for function execution\n- **Address Encoding**: 32-bit value with 2-bit type + 24-bit index\n- **Validated Path**: Compiler verified types match, uses optimized bytecode\n- **Unvalidated Path**: Runtime type checking on each operation\n- **Instruction Args**: Special multi-operand format for calls and constructs\n- **Variant**: Godot's universal type that can hold any value\n- **MethodBind**: Wrapper around C++ methods for script access\n- **Opcode**: Single bytecode instruction (enum value)\n\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":11,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2444,"cache_read_input_tokens":50278,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2444,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"95423b30-b735-4536-8774-5324a45ca4f4","uuid":"1bf998c8-091b-4bce-8773-979db02026ba"}

[VM Architecture Overview](#vm-architecture-overview)\n2. [Address Types and Operand Format](#address-types-and-operand-format)\n3. [Complete Opcode Reference](#complete-opcode-reference)\n4. [Call Convention](#call-convention)\n5. [Variable Access Patterns](#variable-access-patterns)\n6. [Type System Interaction](#type-system-interaction)\n\n---\n\n## VM Architecture Overview\n\nThe GDScript VM is a **stack-based bytecode interpreter** with the following characteristics:\n\n### Execution Model\n\n- **Dispatch Method**: Uses computed goto (on GCC/Clang) for opcode dispatch, falling back to switch statements on other compilers\n- **Instruction Pointer (IP)**: Tracks the current position in the bytecode stream (in integers, not bytes)\n- **Stack Frame**: Each function call allocates a stack frame containing:\n  - Local variables (Variant types)\n  - Temporary values\n  - Instruction arguments\n  - Fixed addresses (self, class, nil)\n\n### Stack Layout\n\n```\n[Stack Frame]\n├── Fixed Addresses (3 slots)\n│   ├── ADDR_STACK_SELF (0): Object instance (self)\n│   ├── ADDR_STACK_CLASS (1): GDScript class reference\n│   └── ADDR_STACK_NIL (2): Nil value\n├── Arguments (parameter count)\n├── Local Variables\n└── Temporary Values\n\n[Instruction Arguments Area]\n├── Array of Variant* pointers for current instruction\n└── Used for multi-operand operations\n```\n\n### Key VM Properties\n\n- **Max Call Depth**: 2,048 (prevents stack overflow from infinite recursion)\n- **Address Bits**: 24 bits for address value + 2 bits for address type = 26 bits per address\n- **Variant Storage**: Variants are 24 bytes each on most platforms (12 bytes value + 12 bytes type info)\n\n---\n\n## Address Types and Operand Format\n\n### Address Type Classification\n\nEach operand in a bytecode instruction contains an address that specifies where data is located:\n\n```c\nenum Address {\n    ADDR_BITS = 24,                        // 24 bits for the address value\n    ADDR_MASK = ((1 << ADDR_BITS) - 1),   // Lower 24 bits = address index\n    ADDR_TYPE_MASK = ~ADDR_MASK,           // Upper bits = type\n\n    ADDR_TYPE_STACK = 0,      // Local variable, parameter, or temporary\n    ADDR_TYPE_CONSTANT = 1,   // Literal constant value\n    ADDR_TYPE_MEMBER = 2,     // Instance member variable\n    ADDR_TYPE_MAX = 3,        // Maximum number of address types\n};\n```\n\n### Address Encoding\n\nEach address operand is a 32-bit integer:\n\n```\n[Type (2 bits)] [Index (24 bits)]\n ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^  ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^\n Upper bits      Lower 24 bits\n\nType Field:\n  0b00 = ADDR_TYPE_STACK     (stack slot, constant, or temporary)\n  0b01 = ADDR_TYPE_CONSTANT  (constant pool reference)\n  0b10 = ADDR_TYPE_MEMBER    (instance member field)\n  0b11 = Reserved\n```\n\n### Extraction in VM\n\n```c\nint address = code_ptr[ip + offset];\nint address_type = (address & ADDR_TYPE_MASK) >> ADDR_BITS;\nint address_index = address & ADDR_MASK;\nVariant *target = &variant_addresses[address_type][address_index];\n```\n\n### Fixed Stack Addresses\n\nThese addresses are reserved and always present:\n\n```c\nenum FixedAddresses {\n    ADDR_STACK_SELF = 0,    // Slot 0: \"self\" (the instance)\n    ADDR_STACK_CLASS = 1,   // Slot 1: Class/Script object\n    ADDR_STACK_NIL = 2,     // Slot 2: nil literal\n    FIXED_ADDRESSES_MAX = 3,\n};\n\n// Convenience encodings\nADDR_SELF = ADDR_STACK_SELF | (ADDR_TYPE_STACK << ADDR_BITS)\nADDR_CLASS = ADDR_STACK_CLASS | (ADDR_TYPE_STACK << ADDR_BITS)\nADDR_NIL = ADDR_STACK_NIL | (ADDR_TYPE_STACK << ADDR_BITS)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Complete Opcode Reference\n\nThe GDScript bytecode instruction set contains **152 opcodes** (0-151).\n\n### Opcode Layout in Memory\n\nMost instructions follow this pattern:\n\n```\ncode[ip + 0]: Opcode (enum value)\ncode[ip + 1]: First operand address\ncode[ip + 2]: Second operand address (if needed)\ncode[ip + 3]: Third operand address (if needed)\ncode[ip + 4+]: Additional fields (operator type, method index, etc.)\n\nInstruction pointer advances by: 2 + number_of_operands + extra_data_size\n```\n\n### 1. Arithmetic & Type Operators\n\n#### `OPCODE_OPERATOR` (0)\n**Unvalidated binary operator (with type specialization)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_OPERATOR\ncode[ip+1]: operand_a (address)\ncode[ip+2]: operand_b (address)\ncode[ip+3]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+4]: operator_type (Variant::Operator enum)\ncode[ip+5]: cached_signature (operand types from last call)\ncode[ip+6]: cached_return_type\ncode[ip+7..7+PTR_SIZE]: cached_operator_function pointer\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Performs operations like +, -, *, /, %, ==, !=, <, >, <=, >=, etc.\n\n**Optimization**: On first execution, caches the operand type signature and operator function. On subsequent calls, if operand types match the cached signature, uses the fast path.\n\n**Sources**:\n- `modules/gdscript/gdscript_vm.cpp:691-776`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_OPERATOR_VALIDATED` (1)\n**Pre-validated binary operator (faster path)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_OPERATOR_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: operand_a (address)\ncode[ip+2]: operand_b (address)\ncode[ip+3]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+4]: operator_func_index (index into _operator_funcs_ptr)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Performs the same operations as OPCODE_OPERATOR but without runtime type checking.\n\n**When Used**: The compiler has verified operand types at compile time.\n\n**Sources**:\n- `modules/gdscript/gdscript_vm.cpp:778-793`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CAST_TO_BUILTIN` (28)\n**Cast to builtin type**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CAST_TO_BUILTIN\ncode[ip+1]: source_value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+3]: target_type (Variant::Type enum)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Converts a Variant to a specific builtin type (int, float, Vector3, etc.)\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CAST_TO_NATIVE` (29)\n**Cast to native Godot class**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CAST_TO_NATIVE\ncode[ip+1]: source_value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+3]: native_type_name_index (in _global_names_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CAST_TO_SCRIPT` (30)\n**Cast to GDScript or Script class**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CAST_TO_SCRIPT\ncode[ip+1]: source_value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address - should be a Script object)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. Type Testing (isinstance checks)\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_BUILTIN` (2)\n**Test if value is a specific builtin type**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_BUILTIN\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, stores bool)\ncode[ip+2]: value_to_test (address)\ncode[ip+3]: builtin_type (Variant::Type enum)\n```\n\n**Example**: `if value is int:`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_ARRAY` (3)\n**Test if value is a typed array**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, stores bool)\ncode[ip+2]: value_to_test (address)\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address)\ncode[ip+4]: element_builtin_type (Variant::Type)\ncode[ip+5]: element_native_type_index (_global_names_ptr index)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Tests both array type and element type: `if value is Array[int]:`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_NATIVE` (4)\n**Test inheritance from native class**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_NATIVE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, stores bool)\ncode[ip+2]: value_to_test (address)\ncode[ip+3]: native_type_index (_global_names_ptr index)\n```\n\n**Example**: `if node is Node2D:`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_SCRIPT` (5)\n**Test inheritance from GDScript class**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_SCRIPT\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, stores bool)\ncode[ip+2]: value_to_test (address)\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address - should be Script)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 3. Variable Assignment\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN` (20)\n**Copy assignment**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `dst = src`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_NULL` (21)\n**Assign nil**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_NULL\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `dst = null` (optimized for common case)\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TRUE` (22)\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_FALSE` (23)\n**Assign boolean literals**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_TRUE or OPCODE_ASSIGN_FALSE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_BUILTIN` (24)\n**Assign with type coercion to builtin**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_BUILTIN\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\ncode[ip+3]: target_type (Variant::Type)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Assignment with implicit conversion: `var i: int = \"123\"`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_ARRAY` (25)\n**Assign typed array with element type checking**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address)\ncode[ip+4]: element_builtin_type (Variant::Type)\ncode[ip+5]: element_native_type_index\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_NATIVE` (26)\n#### `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_SCRIPT` (27)\n**Assign with native/script type checking**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_NATIVE or OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_SCRIPT\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\ncode[ip+3]: type_info (address or index)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 4. Member Variable Access\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_MEMBER` (17)\n**Read instance member variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_MEMBER\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: member_index (integer, not address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `tmp = self.member_name`\n\n**Details**: Member index is used directly to access the `GDScriptInstance::members` array.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_MEMBER` (16)\n**Write instance member variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_MEMBER\ncode[ip+1]: member_index (integer)\ncode[ip+2]: value (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `self.member_name = value`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_STATIC_VARIABLE` (19)\n**Read static class variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_STATIC_VARIABLE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: static_var_index (in GDScript::static_variables)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_STATIC_VARIABLE` (18)\n**Write static class variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_STATIC_VARIABLE\ncode[ip+1]: static_var_index\ncode[ip+2]: value (address)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 5. Named Property Access (dictionaries, objects)\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_NAMED` (14)\n**Unvalidated property read**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_NAMED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: property_name_index (_global_names_ptr index)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `tmp = obj.property_name`\n\n**Validation**: Type checked at runtime for each access.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_NAMED_VALIDATED` (15)\n**Pre-validated property read (faster)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_NAMED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: property_name_index\ncode[ip+4]: getter_func_index (in _getters_ptr)\n```\n\n**When Used**: Compiler verified property exists on type.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_NAMED` (12)\n**Unvalidated property write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_NAMED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: property_name_index\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_NAMED_VALIDATED` (13)\n**Pre-validated property write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_NAMED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: property_name_index\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\ncode[ip+4]: setter_func_index (in _setters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 6. Indexed Access (arrays, dictionaries)\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_KEYED` (9)\n**Unvalidated dictionary/array read with dynamic key**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_KEYED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: key (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `tmp = obj[key]` where key is any Variant\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_KEYED_VALIDATED` (10)\n**Pre-validated keyed read**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_KEYED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: key (address)\ncode[ip+4]: keyed_getter_index (in _keyed_getters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_GET_INDEXED_VALIDATED` (11)\n**Pre-validated integer-indexed read (arrays)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_GET_INDEXED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: index (address - should be int)\ncode[ip+4]: indexed_getter_index (in _indexed_getters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_KEYED` (6)\n**Unvalidated dictionary/array write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_KEYED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: key (address)\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_KEYED_VALIDATED` (7)\n**Pre-validated keyed write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_KEYED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: key (address)\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\ncode[ip+4]: keyed_setter_index (in _keyed_setters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_SET_INDEXED_VALIDATED` (8)\n**Pre-validated integer-indexed write**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_SET_INDEXED_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: object (address)\ncode[ip+2]: index (address)\ncode[ip+3]: value (address)\ncode[ip+4]: indexed_setter_index (in _indexed_setters_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 7. Container Construction\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT` (31)\n**Construct builtin type instance**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: constructor_args via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\ncode[ip+3]: target_type (Variant::Type)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `vec3 = Vector3(1.0, 2.0, 3.0)`\n\n**Instruction Args Format**:\n```\ncode[ip+1]: arg_count (integer)\ncode[ip+2]: arg[0] (address)\ncode[ip+3]: arg[1] (address)\n...\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_VALIDATED` (32)\n**Pre-validated constructor with optimizations**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: args via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\ncode[ip+3]: constructor_func_index (in _constructors_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_ARRAY` (33)\n**Construct untyped Array**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: elements via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `arr = [1, 2, 3]`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_TYPED_ARRAY` (34)\n**Construct typed Array with element type**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_TYPED_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: elements via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\ncode[ip+3]: script_type (address)\ncode[ip+4]: element_builtin_type (Variant::Type)\ncode[ip+5]: element_native_type_index\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_DICTIONARY` (35)\n**Construct Dictionary**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_DICTIONARY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: key_value_pairs via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `dict = {\"key\": value, ...}`\n\n**Instruction Args**: Alternating keys and values\n\n---\n\n### 8. Function Calls\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL` (36)\n**Unvalidated dynamic method call**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, for return value)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address, receiver)\ncode[ip+3]: method_name_index (_global_names_ptr index)\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `result = obj.method_name(arg1, arg2, ...)`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_RETURN` (37)\n**Return value from called method (awaitable)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_RETURN\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_name_index\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Like OPCODE_CALL but returns to caller immediately.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_ASYNC` (38)\n**Call method asynchronously (spawns a coroutine)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_ASYNC\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address, for return value)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_name_index\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND` (43)\n**Call MethodBind-wrapped native method (slow path)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_bind_index (in _methods_ptr)\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**When Used**: Native class methods, slow path.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_RET` (44)\n**Call MethodBind with return handling**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_RET\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_bind_index\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_VALIDATED_RETURN` (46)\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_VALIDATED_NO_RETURN` (47)\n**Pre-validated MethodBind call (fast path)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_VALIDATED_*\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: method_bind_index\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**When Used**: Compiler verified the method exists and has correct signature.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_UTILITY` (40)\n**Call unvalidated utility (global) function**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_UTILITY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: utility_func_name_index (_global_names_ptr)\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `result = sin(x)`, `result = abs(value)`, etc.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_UTILITY_VALIDATED` (41)\n**Pre-validated utility function**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_UTILITY_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: utility_func_index (in _utilities_ptr)\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_GDSCRIPT_UTILITY` (42)\n**Call GDScript-specific utility function**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_GDSCRIPT_UTILITY\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: gds_utility_func_index (in _gds_utilities_ptr)\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Example**: `print()`, `assert()`, etc.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_BUILTIN_TYPE_VALIDATED` (42)\n**Call builtin type method (pre-validated)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_BUILTIN_TYPE_VALIDATED\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: object (address)\ncode[ip+3]: builtin_method_index (in _builtin_methods_ptr)\ncode[ip+4]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `string.to_upper()`, `array.push_back(x)`, etc.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_SELF_BASE` (45)\n**Call parent class method**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_SELF_BASE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: method_name_index\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `super.method()`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC` (49)\n**Call static native method**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: method_bind_index\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC_VALIDATED_RETURN` (50)\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC_VALIDATED_NO_RETURN` (51)\n**Pre-validated static native call**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_NATIVE_STATIC_VALIDATED_*\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: method_bind_index\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CALL_BUILTIN_STATIC` (48)\n**Call static builtin type method**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CALL_BUILTIN_STATIC\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: constructor_or_method_index\ncode[ip+3]: arguments via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n### 9. Lambda & Closure\n\n#### `OPCODE_CREATE_LAMBDA` (52)\n**Create anonymous function (closure)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CREATE_LAMBDA\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: lambda_func_index (in _lambdas_ptr)\ncode[ip+3]: capture_vars via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `lambda = func(x): return x + captured_var`\n\n**Captures**: Variables from enclosing scope are copied into lambda.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_CREATE_SELF_LAMBDA` (53)\n**Create lambda capturing self**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_CREATE_SELF_LAMBDA\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: lambda_func_index\ncode[ip+3]: capture_vars via LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n```\n\n---\n\n### 10. Control Flow\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP` (54)\n**Unconditional jump**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP\ncode[ip+1]: target_ip (instruction pointer, absolute)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `goto label`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP_IF` (55)\n**Jump if condition is true**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP_IF\ncode[ip+1]: condition (address)\ncode[ip+2]: target_ip_true (absolute)\ncode[ip+3]: target_ip_false (absolute)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `if (condition) goto true_label; else goto false_label;`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP_IF_NOT` (56)\n**Jump if condition is false**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP_IF_NOT\ncode[ip+1]: condition (address)\ncode[ip+2]: target_ip_false (absolute)\ncode[ip+3]: target_ip_true (absolute)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP_TO_DEF_ARGUMENT` (57)\n**Jump to default argument initialization**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP_TO_DEF_ARGUMENT\ncode[ip+1]: target_ip\n```\n\n**Purpose**: When fewer arguments passed, jump to initialize remaining parameters.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_JUMP_IF_SHARED` (58)\n**Conditional jump for reference counting**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_JUMP_IF_SHARED\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: target_ip (if reference count > 1)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Copy-on-write optimization for containers.\n\n---\n\n### 11. Iteration\n\n#### `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_*` (64-82)\n**Initialize for-in loop iteration**\n\nType-specialized versions for each container type:\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN` (64) - Untyped/generic\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_INT` (65)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_FLOAT` (66)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_VECTOR2` (67)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_VECTOR2I` (68)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_VECTOR3` (69)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_VECTOR3I` (70)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_STRING` (71)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_DICTIONARY` (72)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_ARRAY` (73)\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_PACKED_*_ARRAY` (74-82) - Various packed array types\n- `OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_OBJECT` (83) - Objects with _iterate()\n\n**Common Format**:\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ITERATE_BEGIN_TYPE\ncode[ip+1]: iterator_var (address, stores iteration state)\ncode[ip+2]: container (address)\ncode[ip+3]: jump_target_if_done (absolute IP)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `for item in container:`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_ITERATE_*` (84-102)\n**Continue for-in loop iteration**\n\nCorresponding OPCODE_ITERATE_* variants for each ITERATE_BEGIN_* type.\n\n**Format**:\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ITERATE_TYPE\ncode[ip+1]: iterator_var (address)\ncode[ip+2]: loop_variable (address, stores current item)\ncode[ip+3]: jump_target_if_done (absolute IP)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 12. Await (Coroutines)\n\n#### `OPCODE_AWAIT` (52)\n**Suspend execution waiting for signal/coroutine**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_AWAIT\ncode[ip+1]: signal_or_coroutine (address)\ncode[ip+2]: jump_target_on_resume (absolute IP)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `yield(object, \"signal\")` or `await signal.emitted`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_AWAIT_RESUME` (53)\n**Resume from await (called when signal is emitted)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_AWAIT_RESUME\ncode[ip+1]: result (address, stores resume value)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 13. Return Statements\n\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN` (59)\n**Return from function (no type checking)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_RETURN\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `return value`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_BUILTIN` (60)\n**Return with builtin type coercion**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_BUILTIN\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: return_type (Variant::Type)\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_ARRAY` (61)\n**Return typed array**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_ARRAY\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: script_type (address)\ncode[ip+3]: element_builtin_type (Variant::Type)\ncode[ip+4]: element_native_type_index\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_NATIVE` (62)\n#### `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_SCRIPT` (63)\n**Return native/script typed value**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_*\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: type_info (address or index)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 14. Global Variables\n\n#### `OPCODE_STORE_GLOBAL` (103)\n**Store value to global variable**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_STORE_GLOBAL\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: global_var_index\n```\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_STORE_NAMED_GLOBAL` (104)\n**Store to named global (by string name)**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_STORE_NAMED_GLOBAL\ncode[ip+1]: value (address)\ncode[ip+2]: global_name_index (_global_names_ptr)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 15. Type Adjustment (Implicit Conversions)\n\n#### `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_*` (105-149)\n**Implicit type conversion for all Godot types**\n\nType-specialized versions for each Variant type:\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_BOOL` (105)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_INT` (106)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_FLOAT` (107)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_STRING` (108)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_VECTOR2` (109)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_VECTOR2I` (110)\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_RECT2` (111)\n- ... (up to OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_PACKED_VECTOR4_ARRAY)\n\n**Format**:\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_TYPE\ncode[ip+1]: destination (address)\ncode[ip+2]: source (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Ensure a variable's type matches its declaration.\n\n---\n\n### 16. Debugging & Metadata\n\n#### `OPCODE_ASSERT` (150)\n**Runtime assertion**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_ASSERT\ncode[ip+1]: condition (address)\ncode[ip+2]: message (address)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: `assert(condition, \"message\")`\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_BREAKPOINT` (151)\n**Debugger breakpoint**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_BREAKPOINT\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Triggers debugger if attached.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_LINE` (152)\n**Line number metadata**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_LINE\ncode[ip+1]: line_number (integer)\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Maps bytecode to source line for debugging.\n\n---\n\n#### `OPCODE_END` (153)\n**Marks end of bytecode**\n\n```\ncode[ip+0]: OPCODE_END\n```\n\n**Purpose**: Terminates function execution.\n\n---\n\n## Call Convention\n\n### Stack Frame Layout on Function Call\n\nWhen a GDScript function is called, the VM:\n\n1. **Allocates Stack Frame**:\n   ```\n   alloca_size = sizeof(Variant*) * 3              // Self, class, nil\n              + sizeof(Variant*) * _instruction_args_size  // Instr args\n              + sizeof(Variant) * _stack_size      // Local variables\n   ```\n\n2. **Initializes Fixed Addresses**:\n   ```c\n   stack[ADDR_STACK_SELF] = instance->owner    // \"self\"\n   stack[ADDR_STACK_CLASS] = script              // Class reference\n   stack[ADDR_STACK_NIL] = Variant()             // nil literal\n   ```\n\n3. **Places Arguments on Stack**:\n   ```c\n   for (int i = 0; i < p_argcount; i++) {\n       stack[i + 3] = arguments[i];  // Skip fixed addresses\n   }\n   ```\n\n4. **Initializes Local Variables**:\n   ```c\n   for (int i = p_argcount + 3; i < _stack_size; i++) {\n       new (&stack[i]) Variant();  // Call Variant constructor\n   }\n   ```\n\n5. **Initializes Temporaries** (if any):\n   ```c\n   for (const KeyValue<int, Variant::Type> &E : temporary_slots) {\n       type_init_function_table[E.value](&stack[E.key]);\n   }\n   ```\n\n### Parameter & Return Value Handling\n\n**Parameters**:\n- Stored at stack indices `[3, 3 + argument_count)`\n- Type-checked on entry if function declares types\n- Implicit conversions applied if needed\n\n**Default Arguments**:\n- If `argument_count > provided_argcount`, VM jumps to `OPCODE_JUMP_TO_DEF_ARGUMENT`\n- Code at that location initializes remaining parameters\n\n**Return Value**:\n- Stored in `Variant retvalue`\n- Returned by `OPCODE_RETURN*` instructions\n- Type-coerced if return type declared\n\n### Error Handling\n\n**Call Errors**:\n- `CALL_ERROR_TOO_FEW_ARGUMENTS` - Missing required parameters\n- `CALL_ERROR_TOO_MANY_ARGUMENTS` - Extra arguments passed\n- `CALL_ERROR_INVALID_ARGUMENT` - Type mismatch on parameter\n- `CALL_ERROR_INVALID_METHOD` - Method not found\n\n**Recursion Protection**:\n- `call_depth` counter (thread-local, max 2048)\n- Prevents stack overflow from infinite recursion\n\n### Instruction Arguments\n\nSome instructions (CALL, CONSTRUCT, etc.) use a special instruction arguments format:\n\n```\ncode[ip+1]: arg_count (number of arguments)\ncode[ip+2]: arg[0] address\ncode[ip+3]: arg[1] address\n...\n\nMacro: LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS\n  - Reads arg_count from code[ip+1]\n  - Populates instruction_args[] pointers\n  - Advances ip by 1 to skip arg_count field\n```\n\n**Usage in VM**:\n```c\n#define LOAD_INSTRUCTION_ARGS           \\\n    int instr_arg_count = _code_ptr[ip + 1];    \\\n    for (int i = 0; i < instr_arg_count; i++) { \\\n        GET_VARIANT_PTR(v, i + 1);              \\\n        instruction_args[i] = v;                \\\n    }                                           \\\n    ip += 1;\n```\n\n---\n\n## Variable Access Patterns\n\n### Local Variables\n\n**Declaration**: Variables declared in function scope.\n\n**Bytecode Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nvar x: int = 10\n```\n\nCompiled to:\n```\nOPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_BUILTIN dst=[stack_index] src=[const_10] type=INT\n```\n\n**Memory**: Stored in stack frame at calculated offset.\n\n### Parameters\n\n**Declaration**: Function parameters.\n\n**Storage**: Stack slots `[3, 3 + param_count)`\n\n**Bytecode Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nfunc foo(a: int, b: Vector3):\n    pass\n```\n\nWhen called:\n- `stack[3]` = first argument (a)\n- `stack[4]` = second argument (b)\n- Arguments type-checked on entry\n\n### Member Variables\n\n**Declaration**: Instance member fields.\n\n**Access Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nself.member = value  # Write\nx = self.member       # Read\n```\n\n**Bytecode**:\n- Read: `OPCODE_GET_MEMBER dst=[stack] member_index=[field_index]`\n- Write: `OPCODE_SET_MEMBER member_index=[field_index] src=[stack]`\n\n**Memory**: Stored in `GDScriptInstance::members` array (Vector<Variant>)\n\n### Static Variables\n\n**Declaration**: Class-level static variables.\n\n**Access Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nstatic my_var = 10\nmy_var = 20\n```\n\n**Bytecode**:\n- Read: `OPCODE_GET_STATIC_VARIABLE dst=[stack] static_index=[var_index]`\n- Write: `OPCODE_SET_STATIC_VARIABLE static_index=[var_index] src=[stack]`\n\n**Memory**: Stored in `GDScript::static_variables` (Vector<Variant>)\n\n### Global Variables\n\n**Declaration**: Project globals (autoload singletons, constants).\n\n**Access Pattern**:\n```gdscript\nGLOBAL_VAR = value\nx = GLOBAL_VAR\n```\n\n**Bytecode**:\n- Write: `OPCODE_STORE_GLOBAL value=[stack] global_index=[index]`\n- Read: Compiled as constant folding or looked up at compile time\n\n### Temporary Variables\n\n**Purpose**: Hold intermediate values during expression evaluation.\n\n**Allocation**: `add_temporary()` in compiler\n\n**Lifetime**: From creation until next statement or cleanup point\n\n**Type Tracking**: Each temporary has a `Variant::Type` for optimization\n\n---\n\n## Type System Interaction\n\n### Variant Type Categories\n\nThe VM operates on Godot's `Variant` type system with these categories:\n\n#### Builtin Types\nPrimitive types with value semantics:\n- `BOOL`, `INT`, `FLOAT`, `STRING`\n- `VECTOR2`, `VECTOR3`, `VECTOR4`\n- `RECT2`, `AABB`, `PLANE`\n- `QUATERNION`, `TRANSFORM2D`, `TRANSFORM3D`, `PROJECTION`, `BASIS`\n- `COLOR`\n- `ARRAY`, `DICTIONARY` (container types)\n- `PACKED_BYTE_ARRAY`, `PACKED_INT32_ARRAY`, `PACKED_INT64_ARRAY`\n- `PACKED_FLOAT32_ARRAY`, `PACKED_FLOAT64_ARRAY`\n- `PACKED_STRING_ARRAY`, `PACKED_VECTOR2_ARRAY`\n- `PACKED_VECTOR3_ARRAY`, `PACKED_COLOR_ARRAY`, `PACKED_VECTOR4_ARRAY`\n\n#### Reference Types\nObject references with reference counting:\n- `OBJECT` - Generic Object pointer\n- `CALLABLE` - Function reference\n- `SIGNAL` - Signal reference\n- `STRING_NAME` - Interned string\n- `NODE_PATH` - Scene tree path\n- `RID` - Resource ID\n\n### Type Checking\n\n**Three-Level Validation**:\n\n1. **No Validation** (OPCODE_OPERATOR, OPCODE_CALL):\n   - Type checking done at runtime for each operation\n   - Slower but handles dynamic code\n\n2. **Compile-Time Validation** (OPCODE_OPERATOR_VALIDATED, OPCODE_CALL_METHOD_BIND_VALIDATED_*):\n   - Compiler verifies types match declarations\n   - Generates optimized bytecode with function pointers\n   - No runtime type checking needed\n\n3. **Implicit Conversion** (OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_*, OPCODE_TYPE_ADJUST_*):\n   - Automatic conversion between compatible types\n   - Example: `int x = 3.14` converts float to int\n\n### Type Annotation Storage\n\n**GDScriptDataType Structure**:\n```c\nstruct GDScriptDataType {\n    Kind kind;                    // BUILTIN, NATIVE, SCRIPT, GDSCRIPT\n    bool has_type;                // Was a type annotation present?\n    Variant::Type builtin_type;   // For BUILTIN kind\n    StringName native_type;       // For NATIVE kind\n    Script *script_type;          // For SCRIPT/GDSCRIPT kind\n    Vector<GDScriptDataType> container_element_types;  // For typed containers\n};\n```\n\n**Storage Locations**:\n- **Function parameters**: `GDScriptFunction::argument_types` (Vector<GDScriptDataType>)\n- **Return type**: `GDScriptFunction::return_type` (GDScriptDataType)\n- **Local variables**: Tracked during compilation in CodeGen\n\n### Typed Array Support\n\nArrays can have element types:\n\n**Declaration**:\n```gdscript\nvar arr: Array[Vector3]\nvar arr: Array[MyClass]\n```\n\n**Bytecode Operations**:\n- `OPCODE_CONSTRUCT_TYPED_ARRAY` - Create with type\n- `OPCODE_ASSIGN_TYPED_ARRAY` - Assign with validation\n- `OPCODE_TYPE_TEST_ARRAY` - Test element type\n- `OPCODE_RETURN_TYPED_ARRAY` - Return with type coercion\n\n**Storage**:\n- Type info: `container_element_types[0]` in GDScriptDataType\n- Element builtin type, native type, or script type\n\n### Operator Validation\n\n**Dynamic Operator Resolution**:\n```c\n// OPCODE_OPERATOR flow:\n1. Check if signature cached (code[ip+5] != 0)\n2. If not cached:\n   - Get operand types: a->get_type(), b->get_type()\n   - Look up validated operator: Variant::get_validated_operator_evaluator(op, a_type, b_type)\n   - Initialize return value with correct type\n   - Cache signature and function pointer\n3. If cached and matches:\n   - Use fast path with cached function pointer\n4. If cached but doesn't match:\n   - Fall back to slower Variant::evaluate()\n```\n\n**Supported Operators**:\n- Arithmetic: `+`, `-`, `*`, `/`, `%`\n- Comparison: `==`, `!=`, `<`, `>`, `<=`, `>=`\n- Logical: `and`, `or`, `not`\n- Bitwise: `&`, `|`, `^`, `~`, `<<`, `>>`\n- Container: `in`, `[]` (indexing)\n- String: `+` (concatenation)\n- Type: `is` (type testing)\n\n---\n\n## Source Files Reference\n\nAll information in this document comes from analyzing the following Godot Engine source files:\n\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_function.h`** - Opcode enum, address types, GDScriptFunction class\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_vm.cpp`** - VM interpreter implementation, opcode dispatch, instruction execution\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_compiler.h`** - CodeGen structure for bytecode generation\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_byte_codegen.h`** - Bytecode generator implementation\n- **`modules/gdscript/gdscript_compiler.cpp`** - Compiler logic\n\nSource: **Godot Engine 4.3-stable**\nhttps://godotengine.org\n\n---\n\n## Terminology\n\n- **IP (Instruction Pointer)**: Current position in bytecode stream (in integers)\n- **Stack Frame**: Memory region allocated for function execution\n- **Address Encoding**: 32-bit value with 2-bit type + 24-bit index\n- **Validated Path**: Compiler verified types match, uses optimized bytecode\n- **Unvalidated Path**: Runtime type checking on each operation\n- **Instruction Args**: Special multi-operand format for calls and constructs\n- **Variant**: Godot's universal type that can hold any value\n- **MethodBind**: Wrapper around C++ methods for script access\n- **Opcode**: Single bytecode instruction (enum value)\n\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
